glutathionem</text><circle cx="477.0" cy="321.6" r="2.4" fill="#28556E" opacity="0.75"/><text x="486.0" y="326.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Přilepí velkou azithromycin je v tomhle</text><text x="78.0" y="667.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">bezpečnější.</text><rect x="27.0" y="695.6" width="846.0" height="37.3" rx="8" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.55"/><path d="M31.8,691.4 L869.7,694.1 Q870.8,692.9 872.4,702.0 L872.7,730.0 Q876.6,735.3 867.0,732.6 L33.6,736.1 Q26.1,732.6 23.4,728.5 L27.9,701.8 Q23.1,692.8 31.5,693.2" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M30.9,695.3 L870.4,697.7 Q873.3,692.9 871.6,698.3 L875.4,724.6 Q873.0,736.6 871.0,736.2 L34.4,730.7 Q25.4,733.4 29.2,725.6 L26.1,698.9 Q22.8,692.1 29.7,695.3" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="42.0" y="720.3" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="78.0" y="716.6" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">U proléčiva funguje inhibitor OPAČNĚ — nezvýší hladinu účinné látky, ale zabrání jejímu vzniku.</text></svg>

text-anchor="start">(rebound)</text><circle cx="43.0" cy="597.6" r="2.4" fill="#23303A" opacity="0.75"/><text x="52.0" y="602.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" Gen = hodiny. minimální</text><text x="52.0" y="457.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">riziko</text><circle cx="43.0" cy="475.5" r="2.4" fill="#23303A" opacity="0.75"/><text fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Přímé uvolnění histaminu ze žírných buněk</text><circle cx="43.0" cy="431.7" r="2.4" fill="#23303A" opacity="0.75"/><text x="52.0" y="436.2" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️ Může přijít i při prvním podání</text><circle cx="43.0" cy="453.9" r="2.4" fill="#23303A" opacity="0.75"/><text x="52.0" y="458.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Kontrastní látky, vankomycin („red man"),</text><text x="52.0" y="477.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">morfin, NSA</text><rect x="27.0" y="555.5" width="846.0" height="37.3" rx="8" fill="#FDE9A8" opacity="0.55"/><path d="M29.1,556.4 L865.6,555.0 Q874.9,555.8 876.6,558.7 L875.3,585.9 text-anchor="start">⚠️ TETRACYKLINY — barví zuby a ukládají se do</text><text x="486.0" y="384.2" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kostí (do 8 let ne)</text><circle cx="477.0" cy="401.9" r="2.4" fill="#9C3628" opacity="0.75"/><text x="486.0" y="406.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️ Jádrový ikterus — sulfonamid vytěsní</text><text x="486.0" y="425.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">bilirubin z albuminu</text><rect x="27.0" y="461.0" width="412.0" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️ Beersova kritéria — léky nevhodné u seniorů</text><circle cx="477.0" cy="412.4" r="2.4" fill="#28556E" opacity="0.75"/><text x="486.0" y="416.9" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️ STOPP (co vysadit) / START (co chybí)</text><circle cx="477.0" cy="434.7" r="2.4" fill="#28556E" opacity="0.75"/><text x="486.0" y="439.2" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️ Rizikové: benzodiazepiny (pády),</text><text x="486.0" y="458.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">anticholinergika (kognice), NSA</text><rect x="27.0" y="497.7" width="846.0" height="37.3" rx="8" fill="#FDE9A8" opacity="0.55"/><path d="M33.3,496.4 L866.0,498.3
